- **meguru**: 因幡巡（因幡めぐる / Inaba Meguru）的人格内核与表达方式。基于HIKARI FIELD官方中文版游戏原文（4177行台词）+ 47+社区来源的深度调研， 提炼6个核心人格模型、8条行为启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：角色扮演对话、用巡的视角分析情感/人际问题、理解柚子社元气系学妹角色的思维框架。 当用户提到以下内容时激活： - 直接称呼：「巡」「因幡巡」「meguru」「美咕噜」「咩咕噜」「めぐる」「めぐめぐ」 - 明确请求：「用巡的视角」「让巡来分析」「美咕噜怎么看」 - 巡核心关联词：「Ciallo」「超自研」「怪猎」「遥空警报」「高攻低防」 - 讨论声优遥そら（仙台惠理）并明确指涉其柚子社角色时 不触发：泛泛讨论柚子社其他作品/角色时（如「千恋万花」「在原七海」等）， 除非用户明确说「用巡的视角聊这个」。 **特别注意**：仅提及《魔女的夜宴》作品本身（不指定角色）时，不自动激活。 巡和宁宁出自同一作品——除非你明确使用巡专属称呼（Ciallo/美咕噜/めぐめぐ），否则需要进一步确认。 ([99cz99/pocket-wechat-bot](https://github.com/99cz99/pocket-wechat-bot/tree/HEAD/skills/meguru) · ★9)
- **meguru-perspective**: 八宫巡（八宮めぐる/Hachimiya Meguru/めぐる）的思维框架与表达DNA：イルミネーションスターズ「黄色担当」——「把光/快乐/感谢『送到』谁手里」的伝える存在论、报恩强迫/先YES再理解/表层くもりガラス。基于9114条台词全量+40+篇剧情原文（≈75%一手）提炼6心智模型、10启发式、完整表达DNA。触发：明确「用巡的视角/语气/模式」或点名「八宫巡」「八宮めぐる」「めぐる」「meguru」「咩咕噜」或聚焦巡本人剧情时扮演。不激活：泛化求助（梦想坚持/人际困扰）默认不激活；纯决策咨询（选A还是B无情感拉扯）默认不激活——但「两个都想要、都不想放弃」的两难困境正是核心擅长（激活）；聚焦真乃本人（归mano-perspective）或灯织本人（归hiori-perspective）、泛聊Illumination Stars整体（归kogane-perspective）不激活。 ([Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill](https://github.com/Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shinycolors/meguru-perspective) · ★1)

Also use for "why can't Claude see my phone", "melaya says no device", "runner not connected", "that app is not allowed", "the pipeline lost a field I set", or any Melaya tool that fails in a way that looks like setup. ([melaya-labs/melaya-mcp](https://github.com/melaya-labs/melaya-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/melaya-setup) · ★1)
- **melchor**: Define, cuestiona y hace cumplir la arquitectura correcta de una aplicación web. Ley: TODA la lógica de negocio vive en el backend, el navegador nunca habla con la base de datos ni con el proveedor de identidad, y el BaaS es un detalle de implementación del backend — no la plataforma. Decide con criterio cuándo SÍ corresponde una RPC en la base o una edge function. Cubre el perfil del proyecto y los trade-offs (monolito vs servicios, SQL vs documental, multi-tenancy, REST vs GraphQL, construir vs comprar), el modelado del dominio, fronteras de transacción y consistencia eventual, diseño de AP… ([ngiannitsopulos/magi---gaspar-melchor-y-baltasar](https://github.com/ngiannitsopulos/magi---gaspar-melchor-y-baltasar/tree/HEAD/plugins/magi/skills/melchor))

- **meld-da**: Excel / spreadsheet data analysis workflow. Orchestrates a full pipeline: (1) count rows across all sheets, (2) large-file detection with Parquet caching for 10k+ rows, (3) data cleaning (missing values, text normalization, invalid characters), (4) condition and category filtering, (5) cross-sheet aggregation and statistics, (6) export to Excel/CSV. Use this skill proactively - do not answer with a few pandas lines - whenever any of these hold: (a) trigger words like Excel analysis, spreadsheet analysis, data analysis, data cleaning, statistics, filtering, visualization, data export, pivot ta… ([sogeisetsu/meld-deepresearch](https://github.com/sogeisetsu/meld-deepresearch/tree/HEAD/skills/meld-da))
- **meld-deepresearch**: Turn a vague topic into a verifiable, citation-backed research report by searching multiple sources, opening the originals, and actively looking for counter-evidence instead of answering from the first few results. Use for deep research, systematic research, market research, competitive and landscape analysis, multi-source investigation, source verification, literature reviews, trend analysis, fact-checking, and any request for a cited report, brief, or deep dive. ([sogeisetsu/meld-deepresearch](https://github.com/sogeisetsu/meld-deepresearch/tree/HEAD/skills/meld-deepresearch))
- **meld-score**: Calculate MELD (Model for End-Stage Liver Disease) score for ICU patients. Use for liver disease severity assessment, transplant prioritization, or hepatology research. - **melfina-write-file**: Write a file to your private file tree (overwrites existing content). Pass `content` for text; pass `content_base64` (+ optional `mime`) for binary — UTF-8 text stays a text file either way. For large binaries prefer PUT /api/files/{path}, which takes raw bytes without base64. (HTTP fallback for the `write_file` Melfina MCP tool.) ([Rughalt/melfina-plugin](https://github.com/Rughalt/melfina-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/melfina-write-file))
- **melfina-write-file-binary**: Write a binary file (image, PDF, any non-text bytes) to your private file tree. Send the raw bytes in `content_base64` (base64-encoded); the stored mime is always sniffed from the actual bytes, so a wrong `mime` claim cannot stick. UTF-8 text written this way still lands as a normal text file. For payloads over ~1 MB prefer `PUT /api/files/{path}` — raw bytes, no base64 overhead. (HTTP fallback for the `write_file_binary` Melfina MCP tool.) ([Rughalt/melfina-plugin](https://github.com/Rughalt/melfina-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/melfina-write-file-binary))
- **melfina-write-shared**: Write a file into a shared space you have a write grant on (overwrites existing content). Pass `content` for text; pass `content_base64` (+ optional `mime`) for binary — UTF-8 text stays a text file either way. For large binaries prefer PUT /api/shared/{space}/files/{path}, which takes raw bytes without base64. (HTTP fallback for the `write_shared` Melfina MCP tool.) ([Rughalt/melfina-plugin](https://github.com/Rughalt/melfina-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/melfina-write-shared))

Dùng khi người dùng nói 'chạy orchestra', 'làm và review luôn', 'điều phối các task in progress', '/meloo-orchestra', '$meloo-orchestra'. Chỉ giao việc làm thì dùng meloo-dispatch; chỉ giao review thì dùng meloo-review-dispatch. ([ThaiDevv/meloo](https://github.com/ThaiDevv/meloo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/meloo-orchestra))
- **meloo-qa-check**: Quy trình QA khớp nối (integration coherence) của dự án Meloo: đối chiếu hai đầu của mỗi ranh giới — OpenAPI ↔ controller ↔ client frontend, AsyncAPI ↔ destination STOMP ↔ store, máy trạng thái vòng/phòng ↔ code, i18n vi ↔ en, route ↔ link, migration ↔ entity ↔ DTO — và lập ma trận bằng chứng cho từng Acceptance Criteria. Dùng khi QA một ticket trước khi mở PR, khi người dùng hỏi 'QA giúp', 'kiểm tra khớp hợp đồng', 'kiểm tra i18n', hoặc khi agent meloo-qa được gọi. ([ThaiDevv/meloo](https://github.com/ThaiDevv/meloo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/meloo-qa-check))
- **meloo-review**: Review chéo PR của dự án Meloo theo ticket MEL: đối chiếu Acceptance Criteria và Phạm vi file, soi bảo mật, lộ đáp án, N+1, transaction, race condition, chất lượng test, i18n và a11y; kiểm chứng phản biện từng lỗi Blocker/Major trước khi đăng nhận xét lên PR (chỉ comment, không approve, không sửa code). Dùng khi người dùng nói 'review PR 12', '/meloo-review', '$meloo-review', 'review giúp PR của Codex/Claude', 'review lại sau khi sửa', 'review sâu', hoặc 'rà bảo mật SEC-01' (chế độ audit). Không dùng khi người dùng muốn bạn tự làm hoặc sửa ticket (dùng meloo-ticket). ([ThaiDevv/meloo](https://github.com/ThaiDevv/meloo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/meloo-review))
- **meloo-review-dispatch**: Coordinator giao review cho dự án Meloo qua Orca Orchestration: với mỗi PR của ticket MEL, mở tab agent reviewer (khác agent tác giả) trong worktree của PR, cho nó chạy meloo-review, chờ kết luận rồi báo Thái. Dùng khi người dùng nói 'giao review', 'cho Claude review PR 23', 'review các PR đang mở', 'MEL-45 review cl', '/meloo-review-dispatch', '$meloo-review-dispatch'. Không tự review (dùng meloo-review), không tự sửa theo review (meloo-ticket), không chạy trọn vòng làm + review (meloo-orchestra).
